### [1] [From Servers to Sites: Compositional Power Trace Generation of LLM Inference for Infrastructure Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18383)
*Grant Wilkins,Fiodar Kazhamiaka,Ram Rajagopal*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对LLM推理工作负载的数据中心电力建模框架，能够从测量到的电力 traces 学习，并为新的流量条件和服务配置合成电力配置文件，在多个LLM、tensor-parallel设置和GPU代际中实现了低于5%的中位绝对能量误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据中心电力模型未捕捉LLM推理工作负载特性——GPU在计算密集的prefill、低功耗的decode和空闲状态之间快速切换，设施需求取决于这些状态在多个设备上的演变和同步方式。运营商需要细粒度traces进行供应、设施管理和调度，电力公司需要站点级负载曲线进行容量和互连规划。

Method: 提出将LLM推理电力表示为两个组成部分：1) 工作负载驱动的运行状态之间的转换；2) 这些状态内与配置相关的电力分布。基于这一观察，开发了一个从测量traces学习并为新流量条件和服务配置合成电力配置文件的框架。这些traces可从GPU服务器聚合到机架、行和设施级负载曲线。

Result: 在多个LLM、tensor-parallel设置和GPU代际中，对于大多数配置实现了低于5%的中位绝对能量误差，同时保留了时间自相关结构。结果traces支持下游分析，包括超额订阅、电力调制和面向电力的负载表征。

Conclusion: 该框架支持基础设施评估，包括超订阅、电力调制和面向公用事业的负载表征，使扁平化铭牌假设和静态trace重放无法支持的基础设施评估成为可能。

Abstract: Datacenter operators and electrical utilities rely on power traces at different spatiotemporal scales. Operators use fine-grained traces for provisioning, facility management, and scheduling, while utilities use site-level load profiles for capacity and interconnection planning. Existing datacenter power models do not capture LLM inference workloads, in which GPUs shift rapidly among compute-intensive prefill, lower-power decode, and idle states, and facility demand depends on how these states evolve and synchronize across many devices. We show that LLM inference power can be represented compositionally through two components: workload-driven transitions among operating states and configuration-specific power distributions within those states. Building on this observation, we develop a trace-generation framework that learns from measured traces and synthesizes power profiles for new traffic conditions and serving configurations. These traces aggregate from GPU servers to rack-, row-, and facility-scale load profiles at the temporal granularity required by the study.
  Across multiple LLMs, tensor-parallel settings, and GPU generations, our framework achieves median absolute energy error below 5% for most configurations while preserving temporal autocorrelation structure. The resulting traces support downstream analyses including oversubscription, power modulation, and utility-facing load characterization, enabling infrastructure evaluations that flat nameplate assumptions and static trace replay cannot support.

</details>


### [2] [High-Performance Portable GPU Primitives for Arbitrary Types and Operators in Julia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18695)
*Emmanuel Pilliat*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: KernelForge.jl是一个Julia库，通过两层可移植架构在GPU上实现scan、mapreduce和矩阵向量原语，最终在NVIDIA A40和AMD MI300X上达到或超过CUB和cuBLAS的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然Kokkos和RAJA等可移植GPU框架简化了跨架构开发，但在基本并行原语上相对vendor优化的库存在可测量的性能开销。

Method: 提出两层可移植架构：1) KernelIntrinsics.jl提供后端无关的warp级shuffle、内存栅栏和向量化内存访问抽象；2) KernelForge.jl基于这些接口构建高性能算法。

Result: 在NVIDIA A40上，KernelForge.jl的scan和mapreduce性能与CUB相当或更优；在NVIDIA A40和AMD MI300X上，矩阵向量操作的吞吐量与cuBLAS在大多数测试配置下相当。

Conclusion: 证明了可移植JIT编译抽象可以在不牺牲通用性的前提下达到vendor级别的吞吐量。

Abstract: Portable GPU frameworks such as Kokkos and RAJA reduce the burden of cross-architecture development but typically incur measurable overhead on fundamental parallel primitives relative to vendor-optimized libraries. We present KernelForge.jl, a Julia library that implements scan, mapreduce, and matrix-vector primitives through a two-layer portable architecture: KernelIntrinsics.jl provides backend-agnostic abstractions for warp-level shuffles, memory fences, and vectorized memory access, while KernelForge.jl builds high-performance algorithms exclusively on top of these interfaces. Evaluated on an NVIDIA A40 and an AMD MI300X, KernelForge.jl matches or exceeds CUB kernel execution time on scan and mapreduce on the A40, and matches cuBLAS throughput on matrix-vector operations across most tested configurations-demonstrating, as a proof of concept, that portable JIT-compiled abstractions can achieve vendor-level throughput without sacrificing generality.

</details>


### [3] [Literature Study on Operational Data Analytics Frameworks in Large-scale Computing Infrastructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19016)
*Shekhar Suman,Xiaoyu Chu,Alexandru Iosup*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文是一篇文献综述研究，探讨大规模计算基础设施（如HPC系统）的运营数据分析（ODA）能力。到2025年，每年将产生泽字节级的数据，现代大规模计算系统日益复杂，需要细粒度监控和ODA能力来优化效率。研究列出了支持ODA的大规模计算基础设施基本要素，调研了流行ODA框架，并提出一个更全面的ODA框架来扩展现有工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 到2025年，每年将产生泽字节（zettabytes）级的数据。现代大规模计算基础设施（如高性能计算HPC系统）规模和复杂性持续演化，引发了可管理性和可持续性方面的担忧。因此，这些复杂系统需要配备细粒度监控和运营数据分析（ODA）能力来优化其效率。

Method: 1. 文献综述方法，列出支持ODA能力的大规模计算基础设施基本支柱；2. 调研在HPC等环境中运行的流行ODA框架；3. 基于Sherif Sak等人提出的大规模图处理分布式生态系统的各层，提出一个更全面的ODA框架；4. 扩展Netti等人提出的现有ODA框架的功能；5. 将提出的框架与研究的先进框架进行比较以突显创新性。

Result: 提出了一个全面的ODA框架，该框架扩展了现有Netti等人的工作，并与大规模图处理分布式生态系统的各层相匹配。通过与先进框架的比较突出了该框架的创新性，并强调了实施先进ODA框架可观察到的显著运营效率。

Conclusion: 研究旨在提高对ODA领域的认识，强调先进ODA框架实现的显著运营效率，希望引起对该领域广泛研究的关注，并讨论了该领域正在进行的热门研究工作。

Abstract: By 2025, there are zettabytes of data generated every year. The size and complexity of modern large-scale computing infrastructures like High-Performance Computing (HPC) systems continue to evolve and become complex, leaving us wondering about their manageability and sustainability concerns. Because of this reason, those complex systems are provided with fine-grained monitoring and Operational Data Analytics (ODA) capabilities to optimise their efficiency. In this literature study, we list the fundamental pillars of the large-scale computing infrastructures which enable its ODA capabilities, and conduct a study of the popular ODA frameworks operating in various such environments (predominantly HPC). Based on that, we propose a more holistic ODA framework matching the various layers of a large-scale graph-processing distributed ecosystem proposed by Sherif Sak et al, that extends the ODA functionalities presented in an existing novel ODA framework proposed by Netti et al. We compare the holistic ODA framework proposed by us to some of the state-of-the-art frameworks that we study as part of this literature to highlight the novelty, which would hopefully draw more attention to perform extensive research in this field. As part of creating awareness, we highlight the significant operational efficiencies observed as a result of the implementation of the state-of-the-art ODA frameworks to make the study appear beneficial for the readers, and lastly, discuss the trending research work ongoing in this field.

</details>

### [4] [Who Tests the Testers? Systematic Enumeration and Coverage Audit of LLM Agent Tool Call Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18245)
*Xuan Chen,Lu Yan,Ruqi Zhang,Xiangyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了SafeAudit框架，通过基于LLM的测试用例生成器和rule-resistance指标来评估现有LLM agent安全测试基准的完整性，发现了超过20%的现有基准未能检测到的不安全行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试虽然评估agent在多样环境和风险类别下的安全性，但一个根本问题仍未得到解答：现有测试套件有多完整？agent通过基准测试后仍存在哪些不安全交互模式？

Method: 提出两个核心贡献：1）基于LLM的枚举器，通过枚举有效的工具调用工作流和多样用户场景来系统生成测试用例；2）引入rule-resistance指标，一种非语义、定量的度量，从现有基准中提炼出紧凑的安全规则，并识别在这些规则下仍未被覆盖的不安全交互模式。

Result: 在3个基准和12个环境中，SafeAudit发现了超过20%的现有基准未能暴露的残余不安全行为，且覆盖率随测试预算增加而单调增长。

Conclusion: 当前安全评估存在显著的完整性差距，meta-auditing（meta审计）作为基准测试的必要补充，对于agent安全测试至关重要。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly act through external tools, making their safety contingent on tool-call workflows rather than text generation alone. While recent benchmarks evaluate agents across diverse environments and risk categories, a fundamental question remains unanswered: how complete are existing test suites, and what unsafe interaction patterns persist even after an agent passes the benchmark? We propose SafeAudit, a meta-audit framework that addresses this gap through two contributions. First, an LLM-based enumerator that systematically generates test cases by enumerating valid tool-call workflows and diverse user scenarios. Second, we introduce rule-resistance, a non-semantic, quantitative metric that distills compact safety rules from existing benchmarks and identifies unsafe interaction patterns that remain uncovered under those rules. Across 3 benchmarks and 12 environments, SafeAudit uncovers more than 20% residual unsafe behaviors that existing benchmarks fail to expose, with coverage growing monotonically as the testing budget increases. Our results highlight significant completeness gaps in current safety evaluation and motivate meta-auditing as a necessary complement to benchmark-based agent safety testing.

</details>


### [5] [Can LLMs Reason Like Automated Theorem Provers for Rust Verification? VCoT-Bench: Evaluating via Verification Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18334)
*Zichen Xie,Wenxi Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了VCoT-Lift框架和VCoT-Bench基准，用于评估大语言模型在Rust程序验证中的推理能力。现有的评估方法仅将验证视为黑盒，仅通过二元通过/失败结果来评估模型，而VCoT-Lift将低层求解器推理提升为高层人类可读的验证步骤，从而能够细粒度地评估模型对验证过程的理解程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型越来越多地辅助安全软件开发，模型是否能满足Rust程序验证的严格要求仍不明确。现有评估将Rust验证视为黑盒，仅通过二元通过/失败结果评估，这无法揭示模型是否真正理解验证所需的逻辑推理。

Method: 1) 提出VCoT-Lift框架，将低层求解器推理转换为高层人类可读的验证链式思维(VCoT)；2) 构建VCoT-Bench基准，包含1,988个VCoT completion任务；3) 从三个正交维度评估：缺失证明的鲁棒性、不同证明类型的能力、以及证明位置的敏感性。

Result: 对10个最先进的模型进行评估，结果显示当前LLM存在严重的脆弱性，在验证推理能力方面远不及自动化定理证明器。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在Rust程序验证任务中缺乏真正的逻辑推理理解能力，无法达到自动化定理证明器所展现的推理水平。需要更细粒度的评估方法来揭示模型的真实能力。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly assist secure software development, their ability to meet the rigorous demands of Rust program verification remains unclear. Existing evaluations treat Rust verification as a black box, assessing models only by binary pass or fail outcomes for proof hints. This obscures whether models truly understand the logical deductions required for verifying nontrivial Rust code. To bridge this gap, we introduce VCoT-Lift, a framework that lifts low-level solver reasoning into high-level, human-readable verification steps. By exposing solver-level reasoning as an explicit Verification Chain-of-Thought, VCoT-Lift provides a concrete ground truth for fine-grained evaluation. Leveraging VCoT-Lift, we introduce VCoT-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark of 1,988 VCoT completion tasks for rigorously evaluating LLMs' understanding of the entire verification process. VCoT-Bench measures performance along three orthogonal dimensions: robustness to varying degrees of missing proofs, competence across different proof types, and sensitivity to the proof locations. Evaluation of ten state-of-the-art models reveals severe fragility, indicating that current LLMs fall well short of the reasoning capabilities exhibited by automated theorem provers.

</details>


### [6] [Where are the Hidden Gems? Applying Transformer Models for Design Discussion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18393)
*Lawrence Arkoh,Daniel Feitosa,Wesley K. G. Assunção*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究旨在探索transformer模型在跨域检测软件设计讨论中的性能。研究对比了BERT、RoBERTa、XLNet、LaMini-Flan-T5-77M和ChatGPT-4o-mini五种模型，在Stack Overflow训练并在GitHub artifacts上评估。结果显示不同模型在精度和召回率之间存在明显权衡，ChatGPT-4o-mini表现最佳，而数据增强方法未带来显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件设计决策对重构和现代化至关重要，但检测设计讨论面临标注数据稀缺和高成本问题。先前跨域研究使用传统分类器，transformer模型在此设置下尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在Stack Overflow上微调五种transformer模型（BERT、RoBERTa、XLNet、LaMini-Flan-T5-77M、ChatGPT-4o-mini），在GitHub pull requests、issues和commits上评估效果。同时测试类似词注入数据增强方法。

Result: BERT和RoBERTa跨域召回率强；XLNet精度高但召回率低；ChatGPT-4o-mini召回率最高，整体性能竞争力强；LaMini-Flan-T5-77M是轻量替代方案，精度较强但性能不均衡；类似词注入数据增强未带来有意义提升。

Conclusion: 现代语言模型在检测设计讨论方面具有潜力，但不同模型在精度-召回率权衡上存在差异。未来研究需进一步优化跨域迁移学习策略。

Abstract: Design decisions are at the core of software engineering and appear in Q\&A forums, mailing lists, pull requests, issue trackers, and commit messages. Design discussions spanning a project's history provide valuable information for informed decision-making, such as refactoring and software modernization. Machine learning techniques have been used to detect design decisions in natural language discussions; however, their effectiveness is limited by the scarcity of labeled data and the high cost of annotation. Prior work adopted cross-domain strategies with traditional classifiers, training on one domain and testing on another. Despite their success, transformer-based models, which often outperform traditional methods, remain largely unexplored in this setting. The goal of this work is to investigate the performance of transformer-based models (i.e., BERT, RoBERTa, XLNet, LaMini-Flan-T5-77M, and ChatGPT-4o-mini) for detecting design-related discussions. To this end, we conduct a conceptual replication of prior cross-domain studies while extending them with modern transformer architectures and addressing methodological issues in earlier work. The models were fine-tuned on Stack Overflow and evaluated on GitHub artifacts (i.e., pull requests, issues, and commits). BERT and RoBERTa show strong recall across domains, while XLNet achieves higher precision but lower recall. ChatGPT-4o-mini yields the highest recall and competitive overall performance, whereas LaMini-Flan-T5-77M provides a lightweight alternative with stronger precision but less balanced performance. We also evaluated similar-word injection for data augmentation, but unlike prior findings, it did not yield meaningful improvements. Overall, these results highlight both the opportunities and trade-offs of using modern language models for detecting design discussion.

</details>


### [7] [SQL-Commenter: Aligning Large Language Models for SQL Comment Generation with Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18606)
*Lei Yu,Peng Wang,Jingyuan Zhang,Xin Wang,Jia Xu,Li Yang,Changzhi Deng,Jiajia Ma,Fengjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SQL-Commenter是一种基于LLaMA-3.1-8B的SQL注释自动生成方法，通过构建复杂SQL查询数据集、持续预训练、监督微调和直接偏好优化(DPO)来提升LLM对SQL语义的理解，在Spider和Bird基准上显著优于现有最佳基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SQL查询理解困难，复杂语法、多样连接类型和深度嵌套带来挑战；现有查询缺乏充足注释，严重阻碍代码可读性、可维护性和知识传递；自动化SQL注释生成面临数据集有限和LLM对SQL特定语义理解不足两大挑战；即使经过持续预训练和监督微调，LLM仍难以掌握复杂SQL语义。

Method: 提出SQL-Commenter方法，包含三个阶段：(1)构建包含复杂SQL查询及专家验证注释的综合数据集；(2)在大型SQL语料上进行持续预训练以增强LLM的语法和语义理解；(3)执行监督微调；(4)引入使用人类反馈的直接偏好优化(DPO)，利用基于偏好的损失函数倾向优选输出，增强细粒度语义学习和上下文依赖的质量评估。

Result: 在Spider和Bird基准上评估，SQL-Commenter显著优于最先进的基线模型；相比最强基线(Qwen3-14B)，在BLEU-4、METEOR和ROUGE-L上分别提升9.29、4.99和13.23个百分点；人类评估表明SQL-Commenter生成的注释在正确性、完整性和自然性方面具有更高质量。

Conclusion: SQL-Commenter通过结合持续预训练、监督微调和DPO偏好优化，有效解决了LLM对复杂SQL语义理解不足的问题，显著提升了SQL注释生成的质量，为代码可维护性和知识传递提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: SQL query comprehension is a significant challenge due to complex syntax, diverse join types, and deep nesting. Many queries lack adequate comments, severely hindering code readability, maintainability, and knowledge transfer. Automated SQL comment generation faces two main challenges: limited datasets that inadequately represent complex real-world queries, and Large Language Models' (LLMs) insufficient understanding of SQL-specific semantics. Our empirical analysis shows that even after continual pre-training and supervised fine-tuning, LLMs struggle with complex SQL semantics, yielding inaccurate comments. To address this, we propose SQL-Commenter, an advanced method based on LLaMA-3.1-8B. We first construct a comprehensive dataset of complex SQL queries with expert-verified comments. Next, we perform continual pre-training on a large SQL corpus to enhance the LLM's syntax and semantic understanding, followed by supervised fine-tuning. Finally, we introduce Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) using human feedback. SQL-Commenter utilizes a preference-based loss function to favor preferred outputs, enhancing fine-grained semantic learning and context-dependent quality assessment. Evaluated on the Spider and Bird benchmarks, SQL-Commenter significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. On average, it surpasses the strongest baseline (Qwen3-14B) by 9.29, 4.99, and 13.23 percentage points on BLEU-4, METEOR, and ROUGE-L, respectively. Moreover, human evaluation demonstrates the superior quality of comments generated by SQL-Commenter in terms of correctness, completeness, and naturalness.

</details>


### [8] [Green Architectural Tactics in ML-enabled Systems: An LLM-based Repository Mining Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18734)
*Vincenzo De Martino,Silverio Martínez-Fernández,Fabio Palomba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究调查了205个GitHub开源ML项目中绿色AI实践的实施情况，并利用大语言模型识别出9个先前未记录的环境可持续实践，为减少ML系统的环境影响提供实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习和人工智能技术的广泛应用引发了对环境可持续性的担忧。Green AI旨在提高效率同时不牺牲准确性。虽然先前研究提出了可持续实践目录，但对其实际采用情况以及是否存在其他未记录实践的了解有限。

Method: 对GitHub上205个开源ML项目进行软件仓库挖掘研究。设计了一种基于大语言模型(LLM)的新机制，能够从代码仓库中识别已知和新的可持续实践。

Result: 研究结果证实了文献中报道的绿色战术在实践中确实被使用，尽管采用率各不相同。此外，基于LLM的方法揭示了9个先前未记录的可持续实践。每个实践都有代码示例支持以促进采用和集成。

Conclusion: 为寻求减少ML系统环境影响的从业者提供见解，为未来可持续实践的自动检测和采用研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Context: The increasing adoption of machine learning (ML) and artificial intelligence (AI) technologies raises growing concerns about their environmental sustainability. Developing and deploying ML-enabled systems is computationally intensive, particularly during training and inference. Green AI has emerged to address these issues by promoting efficiency without sacrificing accuracy. While prior research has proposed catalogs of sustainable practices (i.e., green tactics), there remains limited understanding of their adoption in practice and whether additional, undocumented tactics exist. Objective: This study aims to investigate the extent to which existing sustainable practices are implemented in real-world ML-enabled systems and to identify previously undocumented practices that support environmental sustainability. Method: We conduct a mining software repository study on 205 open-source ML projects on GitHub. To support our analysis, we design a novel mechanism based on large language models (LLMs) capable of identifying both known and new sustainable practices from code repositories. Results: Our findings confirm that green tactics reported in the literature are used in practice, although adoption rates vary. Furthermore, our LLM-based approach reveals nine previously undocumented sustainable practices. Each tactic is supported with code examples to aid adoption and integration. Conclusions: We finally provide insights for practitioners seeking to reduce the environmental impact of ML-enabled systems and offer a foundation for future research in automating the detection and adoption of sustainable practices.

</details>


### [9] [SpaceTime Programming: Live and Omniscient Exploration of Code and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18735)
*Jean-Baptiste Döderlein,Djamel Eddine Khelladi,Mathieu Acher,Benoit Combemale*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SpaceTime Programming（时空编程）方法，统一了探索式编程、实时编程和全知调试三种范式，通过追踪机制同时捕获执行状态和代码变更，使开发者能在空间（代码变体）和时间（执行流）两个维度探索程序。实现了一个Python库SpaceTimePy，并在实时全知调试器和Pygame游戏开发工具中进行了应用验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程环境通常将静态代码与程序动态执行分离，开发者需要在编写代码和观察执行之间切换，但现有工具难以理解代码变更与运行时行为之间的关系。现有方案（探索式编程、实时编程、全知调试）往往只关注特定方面，缺乏一个集多种能力于一体的完整集成环境。

Method: 核心方法是设计一种追踪机制（trace mechanism），不仅捕获执行状态，还同时记录对应的代码变更，从而支持在空间维度（代码变体）和时间维度（执行流）上探索程序。实现了Python库SpaceTimePy作为概念验证。

Result: 在5个真实Python项目上进行了评估，测试套件的性能开销范围为35%至150%。

Conclusion: SpaceTime Programming成功统一了多种编程范式，创建了可同时探索代码修改和执行流的编程模型，证明了在调试和游戏开发等场景中的可行性，但存在一定的性能开销需要进一步优化。

Abstract: Programming environments typically separate the world of static code from the dynamic execution of programs. Developers must switch between writing code and observing its execution, often with limited tools to understand the relationship between code changes and runtime behavior. Several paradigms and approaches exist to bridge this gap, including exploratory programming for comparing code variants, live programming for immediate feedback, and omniscient debugging for exploring execution history. However, existing solutions tend to focus on specific aspects and one specific paradigm rather than providing a fully integrated environment with multiple capabilities. This paper introduces \spacetime Programming, a novel approach that unifies these paradigms to create a programming model for exploring both code modifications and execution flow. At the core of our approach is a trace mechanism that captures not only execution state but also the corresponding code changes, enabling developers to explore programs in both space (code variants) and time (execution flow). As a proof of concept, we implemented a Python library supporting SpaceTime Programming and applied it in two contexts: a live omniscient debugger and a Pygame game development tool, showcased through a Flappy Bird-like game. We further evaluated SpaceTimePy on five real-world Python projects, finding performance overhead ranging from 35% to 150% on test suites.

</details>

### [10] [QuaQue: Design and SQL Implementation of Condensed Algebra for Concurrent Versioning of Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18654)
*Jey Puget Gil,Emmanuel Coquery,John Samuel,Gilles Gesquière*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: QuaQue是一个将SPARQL查询翻译为SQL的系统，用于在关系数据库中高效查询多版本知识图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 版本化知识图谱的高效查询是当前面临的重要挑战，特别是在跨多个版本查询数据时。

Method: 提出一种新颖的压缩代数(condensed algebra)，使用位字符串(bitstrings)在关系模型中紧凑存储版本信息，实现从SPARQL代数到SQL的翻译。

Result: 与原生RDF三元组存储相比，QuaQue在性能上展现出显著优势，验证了其可行性和性能收益。

Conclusion: 通过将版本化知识图谱查询问题转化为关系数据库中的SQL查询，QuaQue/ConVer-G系统提供了一种可行且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The management of versioned knowledge graphs presents significant challenges, particularly in querying data across multiple versions efficiently. This paper introduces QuaQue, a key component of the ConVer-G system, which addresses this challenge by translating SPARQL (SPARQL Protocol and RDF Query Language) queries into SQL (Structured Query Language). QuaQue leverages a novel condensed algebra to operate on a relational model where versioning information is compactly stored using bitstrings. This approach allows for efficient querying of concurrent versions of knowledge graphs within a standard relational database system. We present the key concepts of our condensed algebra, detail the translation process from SPARQL algebra to SQL, and provide a comparative benchmark against a native RDF (Resource Description Framework) triple store, demonstrating the viability and performance benefits of our approach.

</details>


### [11] [Let's Play Tag: Linear Time Evaluation of Conjunctive Queries under TGD Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18709)
*Nofar Carmeli,Carsten Lutz,Marcin Przybyłko*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了约束条件下（由tuple-generating dependencies (TGDs)表示）共查询的线性时间评估极限，涵盖多种评估模式：单测试、全测试、计数、字典序直接访问和枚举。提出了在特定TGD类上lifting已知二分法的approach。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据库理论中，评估共查询的复杂性是一个核心问题。已有研究在无约束情况下建立了二分法（可在线性时间完成或不可完成），但在实际应用中，查询往往受到约束（如TGDs）的限制。本文旨在探索在有约束的情况下，如何将无约束设置中的已知结果进行推广。

Method: 提出了一种方法，能够在特定条件下将无约束设置中的二分法结果提升到约束环境中。具体针对两类TGDs进行分析：(1) 非递归TGDs集合，每个TGD在头部使用最多二元关系符号或最多有两个frontier变量；(2) frontier-guarded full TGDs。

Result: 该方法成功应用于除枚举外的所有评估模式（单测试、全测试、计数、字典序直接访问）。对于这两类TGDs，可以建立类似于无约束情况下的二分法结果。同时，文章提供了示例说明在枚举模式和更宽松的TGD类中面临的挑战。

Conclusion: 完全分类仍遥不可及，但在特定TGD类上可以lifting已知二分法。结果表明，在约束条件下，查询评估的复杂性在很大程度上取决于约束的类型和评估模式。该工作为理解TGD约束下共查询评估的复杂性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the limits of linear time evaluation of conjunctive queries under constraints expressed as tuple-generating dependencies (TGDs), across several modes of query evaluation: single-testing, all-testing, counting, lexicographic direct access, and enumeration. While full classifications seem far beyond reach, we propose an approach that, for some evaluation modes and classes of TGDs, makes it possible to lift known dichotomies from the unconstrained setting. In particular, our approach applies to all mentioned evaluation modes except enumeration, when the constraints fall into one of two classes: non-recursive sets of TGDs in which every TGD uses at most binary relation symbols in the head or has at most two frontier variables; and frontier-guarded full TGDs. We further provide a collection of examples showcasing the challenges that arise for enumeration and for less restrictive classes of TGDs.

</details>

### [12] [Frayed RoPE and Long Inputs: A Geometric Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18017)
*Davis Wertheimer,Aozhong Zhang,Derrick Liu,Penghang Yin,Naigang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了RoPE在长输入下性能崩溃的几何原因：RoPE破坏了key/query点云的聚类分离，抑制了sink token功能。提出RoPE-ID通过对部分通道应用高频RoPE来解决该问题，在LongBench和RULER基准上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决RoPE在输入长度超出训练长度时性能崩溃的问题。已有分析指出长输入会导致通道旋转"超出分布"，但尚不清楚额外旋转如何导致病态行为。

Method: 通过实证和理论分析研究RoPE下注意力行为的几何特性。提出RoPE-ID：对部分通道应用高频RoPE，使注意力层能够泛化到更长的输入。

Result: 发现注意力会导致分离的key和query潜在点云紧密聚类，从而创建sink tokens。RoPE应用于更长输入会损害这种key/query聚类分离，抑制sink token功能。RoPE-ID通过高频RoPE保留聚类结构。

Conclusion: RoPE-ID在1B和3B参数Transformer上，使用LongBench和RULER信息检索基准测试，证明了扩展输入的有效性。

Abstract: Rotary Positional Embedding (RoPE) is a widely adopted technique for encoding position in language models, which, while effective, causes performance breakdown when input length exceeds training length. Prior analyses assert (rightly) that long inputs cause channels to rotate ``out of distribution,'' but it is not clear how extra rotation relates to or causes pathological behavior. Through empirical and theoretical analysis we advance a unified geometric understanding of attention behavior with RoPE. We find that attention induces tight clustering of separated key and query latent point clouds, allowing for creation of sink tokens: placeholders that allow attention heads to avoid token mixing when not required. RoPE applied to longer inputs damages this key/query cluster separation, producing pathological behavior by inhibiting sink token functionality. From this geometric perspective, we propose RoPE-ID (In Distribution), a straightforward modification that allows attention layers to generalize to longer inputs out of the box: apply RoPE with high frequency to a subset of channels. We demonstrate the effectiveness of RoPE-ID for extended inputs using 1B and 3B parameter Transformers on the LongBench and RULER information retrieval benchmarks.

</details>


### [13] [Engineering Verifiable Modularity in Transformers via Per-Layer Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18029)
*J. Clayton Kerce*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Transformer模型中所谓的"Hydra效应"——由于分布式冗余的存在，对关键注意力头的消融无法产生预期的行为改变，使得可解释性研究陷入困境。作者提出了一种结合双流处理、分层监督和门控注意力的新架构，能够有效暴露模型的隐藏模块化结构，使消融效果提升5-23倍，控制杠杆提升4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型对手术式控制具有顽抗性。现有的可解释性方法虽然能通过相关性识别关键组件，但无法预测或控制其因果作用。这种"Hydra效应"使得可解释性研究成为镜花水月——识别出的关键部件在消融后被其他组件补偿，导致行为几乎不变。

Method: 提出一种新型架构，包含三个核心组件：1）双流处理机制，将token表示和上下文表示分离；2）分层监督机制，为每一层提供独立的梯度信号；3）门控注意力机制，将激活模式正则化为离散模式。该架构通过分层监督使模型暴露隐藏的模块化结构。

Result: 1. 消融效果显著增强：使用分层监督训练的模型消融效果是标准训练对照组的5-23倍；2. 控制杠杆提升：针对目标行为的控制能力提升4倍；3. 消融效应分布改变：标准训练消融效应集中在零附近（Winograd标准差0.63%），分层监督后效应广泛分布（标准差6.32%）；4. 功能重组验证：不同任务路由通过不同的注意力头。

Conclusion: 分层监督是揭示Transformer隐藏模块化的关键架构干预。消融效应方差的显著增大不是测量噪声，而是暴露模块化的特征信号。本文建立了将可解释性从被动观察转变为主动控制的方法论。

Abstract: Transformers resist surgical control. Ablating an attention head identified as critical for capitalization produces minimal behavioral change because distributed redundancy compensates for damage. This Hydra effect renders interpretability illusory: we may identify components through correlation, but cannot predict or control their causal role. We demonstrate that architectural interventions can expose hidden modularity. Our approach combines dual-stream processing separating token and contextual representations, per-layer supervision providing independent gradient signal at each depth, and gated attention regularizing toward discrete activation patterns. When trained with per-layer supervision, models produce ablation effects 5 to 23 times larger than architecturally identical controls trained with standard objectives. This enables 4 times greater control leverage on targeted behaviors: scaling identified attention heads produces smooth, predictable changes in model output. The key finding is architectural. Without per-layer supervision, ablation damage concentrates near zero with low variance (Winograd standard deviation 0.63%). With per-layer supervision, effects spread widely (standard deviation 6.32%), revealing which predictions depend on which circuits. The larger variance is not measurement noise but the signature of unmasked modularity. We validate our approach through three components: engineered features that capture computational dynamics rather than vocabulary structure (validated by near-zero correlation with raw activation clustering), an architecture providing positive control for modularity, and causal experiments demonstrating functional reorganization where different tasks route through different attention heads. This es tablishes a methodology for transforming interpretability from passive observation to active control.

</details>


### [14] [Towards Differentiating Between Failures and Domain Shifts in Industrial Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18032)
*Natalia Wojak-Strzelecka,Szymon Bobek,Grzegorz J. Nalepa,Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合改进的Page-Hinkley变点检测器、监督域适应异常检测和可解释人工智能(XAI)组件的方法，用于区分工业系统中的故障与正常的域迁移（domain shift），并通过钢铁厂数据流实验进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，数据分布变化并不总是代表异常系统状态。例如处理新产品时的域迁移是正常演变，但可能被错误识别为故障。区分故障与这种"健康"变化对于确保系统实际鲁棒性至关重要。传统方法无法有效区分这两种情况。

Method: 提出方法包含三个组件：1) 改进的Page-Hinkley变点检测器用于识别域迁移和可能故障；2) 基于监督域适应的算法用于快速在线异常检测；3) XAI组件帮助人工操作员最终区分域迁移和故障。

Result: 通过钢铁厂数据流的实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够不仅检测数据分布变化和异常，还能区分故障与过程本身固有的正常域迁移，为工业系统提供更实用的异常检测解决方案。

Abstract: Anomaly and failure detection methods are crucial in identifying deviations from normal system operational conditions, which allows for actions to be taken in advance, usually preventing more serious damages. Long-lasting deviations indicate failures, while sudden, isolated changes in the data indicate anomalies. However, in many practical applications, changes in the data do not always represent abnormal system states. Such changes may be recognized incorrectly as failures, while being a normal evolution of the system, e.g. referring to characteristics of starting the processing of a new product, i.e. realizing a domain shift. Therefore, distinguishing between failures and such ''healthy'' changes in data distribution is critical to ensure the practical robustness of the system. In this paper, we propose a method that not only detects changes in the data distribution and anomalies but also allows us to distinguish between failures and normal domain shifts inherent to a given process. The proposed method consists of a modified Page-Hinkley changepoint detector for identification of the domain shift and possible failures and supervised domain-adaptation-based algorithms for fast, online anomaly detection. These two are coupled with an explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) component that aims at helping the human operator to finally differentiate between domain shifts and failures. The method is illustrated by an experiment on a data stream from the steel factory.

</details>


### [15] [MST-Direct: Matching via Sinkhorn Transport for Multivariate Geostatistical Simulation with Complex Non-Linear Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18036)
*Tchalies Bachmann Schmitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于最优传输理论的新型多变量地质统计模拟算法MST-Direct，用于在复杂非线性依赖关系下同时匹配多变量分布并保持空间相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高斯Copula和LU分解方法假设线性相关结构，无法忠实重现地质变量间的复杂非线性依赖模式，包括双峰分布、阶跃函数和异方差关系等。

Method: 基于Sinkhorn算法的最优传输方法，将所有变量作为多维向量同时处理，在完整联合空间中进行关系匹配，而非依赖成对线性依赖。

Result: 该方法能够在保持空间相关结构的同时，直接匹配多变量分布。

Conclusion: MST-Direct为复杂非线性多变量地质统计模拟提供了一种新的有效途径，有望克服传统方法在处理双峰分布、阶跃函数和异方差关系时的局限性。

Abstract: Multivariate geostatistical simulation requires the faithful reproduction of complex non-linear dependencies among geological variables, including bimodal distributions, step functions, and heteroscedastic relationships. Traditional methods such as the Gaussian Copula and LU Decomposition assume linear correlation structures and often fail to preserve these complex joint distribution patterns. We propose MST-Direct (Matching via Sinkhorn Transport), a novel algorithm based on Optimal Transport theory that uses the Sinkhorn algorithm to directly match multivariate distributions while preserving spatial correlation structures. The method processes all variables simultaneously as a single multidimensional vector, enabling relational matching across the full joint space rather than relying on pairwise linear dependencies.

</details>


### [16] [Quotient Geometry and Persistence-Stable Metrics for Swarm Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18041)
*Mark M. Bailey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体编队（swarm/constellation）重构的持久性稳定对称性不变几何表示。提出了商形成空间 $\mathcal{S}_n(M,G)=M^n/(G\times S_n)$ 和形成匹配度量 $d_{M,G}$，通过优化环境对称性 $g\in G$ 和重标签 $\sigma\in S_n$ 的最坏情况分配误差来定义。该度量是 Gromov-Hausdorff 距离的结构化、物理可解释的松弛，与 Vietoris-Rips 持久性的稳定性结合可提供重构监控的持久性稳定签名。还分析了度量几何性质，并在相位圆模型中证明了条件逆定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统的编队重构监控是核心问题。传统方法缺乏对对称性（如智能体重标签、环境对称变换）的不变性和持久性稳定性保证。本文旨在提供一种统一的框架来处理这些挑战。

Method: 1. 构造商形成空间 $\mathcal{S}_n(M,G)=M^n/(G\times S_n)$；2. 定义形成匹配度量 $d_{M,G}$，优化最坏情况分配误差；3. 建立与 Gromov-Hausdorff 距离的关系；4. 结合 Vietoris-Rips 持久性稳定性；5. 分析度量几何性质；6. 在相位圆模型中证明逆定理

Result: 1. 建立不等式 $d_{\mathrm{GH}}(X_x,X_y)\le d_{M,G}([x],[y])$；2. 持久性稳定签名 $d_B(Φ_k([x]),Φ_k([y]))\le d_{M,G}([x],[y])$；3. 紧致性/完备性结果；4. 相位圆模型中 $H_0$ 签名在半圆支撑和间隙标记margin条件下局部双Lipschitz于 $d_{M,G}$

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为多智能体编队重构监控提供了数学基础，融合了拓扑数据分析与度量几何，在卫星星座和编队飞行等场景具有应用价值。

Abstract: Swarm and constellation reconfiguration can be viewed as motion of an unordered point configuration in an ambient space. Here, we provide persistence-stable, symmetry-invariant geometric representations for comparing and monitoring multi-agent configuration data. We introduce a quotient formation space $\mathcal{S}_n(M,G)=M^n/(G\times S_n)$ and a formation matching metric $d_{M,G}$ obtained by optimizing a worst-case assignment error over ambient symmetries $g\in G$ and relabelings $σ\in S_n$. This metric is a structured, physically interpretable relaxation of Gromov--Hausdorff distance: the induced inter-agent metric spaces satisfy $d_{\mathrm{GH}}(X_x,X_y)\le d_{M,G}([x],[y])$. Composing this bound with stability of Vietoris--Rips persistence yields $d_B(Φ_k([x]),Φ_k([y]))\le d_{M,G}([x],[y])$, providing persistence-stable signatures for reconfiguration monitoring. We analyze the metric geometry of $(\mathcal{S}_n(M,G),d_{M,G})$: under compactness/completeness assumptions on $M$ and compact $G$ it is compact/complete and the metric induces the quotient topology; if $M$ is geodesic then the quotient is geodesic and exhibits stratified singularities along collision and symmetry strata, relating it to classical configuration spaces. We study expressivity of the signatures, identifying symmetry-mismatch and persistence-compression mechanisms for non-injectivity. Finally, in a phase-circle model we prove a conditional inverse theorem: under semicircle support and a gap-labeling margin, the $H_0$ signature is locally bi-Lipschitz to $d_{M,G}$ up to an explicit factor, yielding two-sided control. Examples on $\mathbb{S}^2$ and $\mathbb{T}^m$ illustrate satellite-constellation and formation settings.

</details>


### [17] [NANOZK: Layerwise Zero-Knowledge Proofs for Verifiable Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18046)
*Zhaohui Geoffrey Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: METHOD是一个零知识证明系统，使LLM推理可验证。用户可以密码学确认输出对应于特定模型的计算，解决了LLM API提供商可能使用更便宜模型或应用激进量化而用户无法检测的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户调用专有LLM API时，无法密码学确认实际使用的是声称的模型。服务提供商可能替换更便宜的模型、应用激进量化或返回缓存响应——这些对支付前沿能力高级费用的用户来说是不可检测的。

Method: 方法利用transformer推理自然分解为独立层计算的特点，建立逐层证明框架，每层产生恒定大小的证明（与模型宽度无关）。开发了非算术操作（softmax、GELU、LayerNorm）的查找表近似，引入零可测量精度损失，并引入Fisher信息引导的验证方案。

Result: 在d=128的transformer模型上，METHOD生成5.5KB的恒定大小层证明（2.1KB注意力 + 3.5KB MLP），验证时间24ms。与EZKL相比，METHOD在d=128时实现70x更小证明和5.7x更快证明时间，同时保持形式化可靠性保证（epsilon < 1e-37）。查找表近似精确保持模型困惑度。

Conclusion: METHOD实现了LLM推理的零知识证明验证，通过逐层框架和查找表近似技术，在保持模型质量的同时提供了可扩展的验证方案，解决了API调用中的信任问题。

Abstract: When users query proprietary LLM APIs, they receive outputs with no cryptographic assurance that the claimed model was actually used. Service providers could substitute cheaper models, apply aggressive quantization, or return cached responses - all undetectable by users paying premium prices for frontier capabilities. We present METHOD, a zero-knowledge proof system that makes LLM inference verifiable: users can cryptographically confirm that outputs correspond to the computation of a specific model.
  Our approach exploits the fact that transformer inference naturally decomposes into independent layer computations, enabling a layerwise proof framework where each layer generates a constant-size proof regardless of model width. This decomposition sidesteps the scalability barrier facing monolithic approaches and enables parallel proving. We develop lookup table approximations for non-arithmetic operations (softmax, GELU, LayerNorm) that introduce zero measurable accuracy loss, and introduce Fisher information-guided verification for scenarios where proving all layers is impractical.
  On transformer models up to d=128, METHOD generates constant-size layer proofs of 5.5KB (2.1KB attention + 3.5KB MLP) with 24 ms verification time. Compared to EZKL, METHOD achieves 70x smaller proofs and 5.7x faster proving time at d=128, while maintaining formal soundness guarantees (epsilon < 1e-37). Lookup approximations preserve model perplexity exactly, enabling verification without quality compromise.

</details>


### [18] [Lightweight Adaptation for LLM-based Technical Service Agent: Latent Logic Augmentation and Robust Noise Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18074)
*Yi Yu,Junzhuo Ma,Chenghuang Shen,Xingyan Liu,Jing Gu,Hangyi Sun,Guangquan Hu,Jianfeng Liu,Weiting Liu,Mingyue Pu,Yu Wang,Zhengdong Xiao,Rui Xie,Longjiu Luo,Qianrong Wang,Gurong Cui,Honglin Qiao,Wenlian Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级适配框架，用于将大型语言模型应用于复杂技术服务领域，解决人类演示中缺乏显式认知链、响应多样性导致的歧义以及标准训练范式的高资源成本等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂技术服务领域适配大型语言模型面临三大挑战：1) 人类演示缺乏显式认知链，难以捕捉潜在决策动态；2) 有效响应的多样性导致固有歧义；3) 标准训练范式的资源和时间成本过高，阻碍实际部署。

Method: 提出三个关键贡献：1) 潜在逻辑增强：通过计划感知轨迹建模和决策推理增强，弥合表面监督与潜在决策逻辑之间的差距；2) 鲁棒降噪：构建多重真值数据集，采用双过滤方法验证多样性响应，捕捉语义多样性；3) 轻量级适配：设计混合奖励机制，融合LLM判断器和轻量级相关性重排序器，以较低计算成本提炼高保真奖励信号。

Result: 在真实云服务任务上的实证评估表明，通过潜在逻辑增强和鲁棒降噪，框架实现了稳定性和性能提升；混合奖励机制实现了与标准LLM-as-a-judge方法相当的alignment效果，同时显著降低训练时间。

Conclusion: 该框架为技术服务智能体的实际部署提供了实用价值，在保证性能的同时大幅降低了计算成本，具有良好的实际应用前景。

Abstract: Adapting Large Language Models in complex technical service domains is constrained by the absence of explicit cognitive chains in human demonstrations and the inherent ambiguity arising from the diversity of valid responses. These limitations severely hinder agents from internalizing latent decision dynamics and generalizing effectively. Moreover, practical adaptation is often impeded by the prohibitive resource and time costs associated with standard training paradigms. To overcome these challenges and guarantee computational efficiency, we propose a lightweight adaptation framework comprising three key contributions. (1) Latent Logic Augmentation: We introduce Planning-Aware Trajectory Modeling and Decision Reasoning Augmentation to bridge the gap between surface-level supervision and latent decision logic. These approaches strengthen the stability of Supervised Fine-Tuning alignment. (2) Robust Noise Reduction: We construct a Multiple Ground Truths dataset through a dual-filtering method to reduce the noise by validating diverse responses, thereby capturing the semantic diversity. (3) Lightweight Adaptation: We design a Hybrid Reward mechanism that fuses an LLM-based judge with a lightweight relevance-based Reranker to distill high-fidelity reward signals while reducing the computational cost compared to standard LLM-as-a-Judge reinforcement learning. Empirical evaluations on real-world Cloud service tasks, conducted across semantically diverse settings, demonstrate that our framework achieves stability and performance gains through Latent Logic Augmentation and Robust Noise Reduction. Concurrently, our Hybrid Reward mechanism achieves alignment comparable to standard LLM-as-a-judge methods with reduced training time, underscoring the practical value for deploying technical service agents.

</details>


### [19] [SLEA-RL: Step-Level Experience Augmented Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Turn Agentic Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18079)
*Prince Zizhuang Wang,Shuli Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLEA-RL是一种用于长程多轮智能体任务的经验增强强化学习框架，通过在每个决策步骤基于当前观察动态检索相关经验来解决静态检索的匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在多轮工具使用任务中仅在初始任务描述时检索一次经验，随着episode推进，静态检索与动态变化的环境观察越来越不匹配，导致性能下降。

Method: 提出三组件框架：(1) 步骤级观察聚类，将结构等效的环境状态分组以实现高效聚类索引检索；(2) 自演进经验库，通过基于分数的 admission 和速率受限提取来提炼成功策略和失败模式；(3) 策略优化采用步骤级信用分配实现多轮episode的细粒度优势估计。经验库通过语义分析而非梯度更新与策略共同演进。

Result: 在长程多轮智能体基准测试上的实验表明，SLEA-RL优于各种强化学习基线方法。

Conclusion: SLEA-RL通过在每个决策步骤动态检索相关经验，显著提升了多轮任务中LLM智能体的性能，为经验增强的强化学习提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown strong results on multi-turn tool-use tasks, yet they operate in isolation during training, failing to leverage experiences accumulated across episodes. Existing experience-augmented methods address this by organizing trajectories into retrievable libraries, but they retrieve experiences only once based on the initial task description and hold them constant throughout the episode. In multi-turn settings where observations change at every step, this static retrieval becomes increasingly mismatched as episodes progress. We propose SLEA-RL (Step-Level Experience-Augmented Reinforcement Learning), a framework that retrieves relevant experiences at each decision step conditioned on the current observation. SLEA-RL operates through three components: (i) step-level observation clustering that groups structurally equivalent environmental states for efficient cluster-indexed retrieval; (ii) a self-evolving experience library that distills successful strategies and failure patterns through score-based admission and rate-limited extraction; and (iii) policy optimization with step-level credit assignment for fine-grained advantage estimation across multi-turn episodes. The experience library evolves alongside the policy through semantic analysis rather than gradient updates. Experiments on long-horizon multi-turn agent benchmarks demonstrate that SLEA-RL achieves superior performance compared to various reinforcement learning baselines.

</details>


### [20] [Enhancing Reinforcement Learning Fine-Tuning with an Online Refiner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18088)
*Hao Ma,Zhiqiang Pu,Yang Liu,Xiaolin Ai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对强化学习微调中约束与优化目标的内在矛盾，提出动态约束方法：利用参考模型作为在线精炼器自动修正输出，使约束强度随输出质量自适应调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束对于稳定强化学习微调(RFT)至关重要，但约束越强越会限制模型发现更好解决方案的能力，存在内在张力。需要解决约束强度与优化目标之间的矛盾。

Method: 提出动态约束方法：使用参考模型作为"在线精炼器"(online refiner)，接收微调模型的输出并生成最小修正版本（保留正确内容，修正错误），然后通过监督微调损失训练模型产生精炼后的输出。

Result: 动态约束自动根据输出质量调整强度/放松程度。实验表明在对话和代码生成任务中，动态约束优于KL正则化和无约束基线，获得更高任务奖励的同时保持训练稳定性。

Conclusion: 动态约束能有效平衡约束强度与模型能力提升，在保持训练稳定性的同时显著提升任务表现，为约束优化提供新思路。

Abstract: Constraints are essential for stabilizing reinforcement learning fine-tuning (RFT) and preventing degenerate outputs, yet they inherently conflict with the optimization objective because stronger constraints limit the ability of a fine-tuned model to discover better solutions. We propose \textit{dynamic constraints} that resolve this tension by adapting to the evolving capabilities of the fine-tuned model based on the insight that constraints should only intervene when degenerate outputs occur. We implement this by using a reference model as an \textit{online refiner} that takes the response from the fine-tuned model and generates a minimally corrected version which preserves correct content verbatim while fixing errors. A supervised fine-tuning loss then trains the fine-tuned model to produce the refined output. This mechanism yields a constraint that automatically strengthens or relaxes based on output quality. Experiments on dialogue and code generation show that dynamic constraints outperform both KL regularization and unconstrained baselines, achieving substantially higher task rewards while maintaining training stability.

</details>


### [21] [Tula: Optimizing Time, Cost, and Generalization in Distributed Large-Batch Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18112)
*Sahil Tyagi,Feiyi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tula，一个用于大规模卷积模型训练的在线自动化服务，通过并行系统建模与统计性能预测相结合，自动优化批次大小，实现时间、成本与收敛质量的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式训练中，简单增加批次大小会导致收益递减（出现拐点），且大批次会带来泛化差距问题。现有方法无法自动确定最优批次大小，需结合模型、数据和计算资源进行优化。

Method: Tula采用并行系统建模与统计性能预测相结合的方法，识别最优批次大小。该服务可预测训练时间和成本。

Result: Tula对训练时间和成本的预测误差为7.5-14%，在多种视觉任务上相比标准大批次训练实现最高20倍加速，测试准确率平均提升9%。

Conclusion: Tula成功同时缓解了泛化差距并加速了训练，为大批次训练的自动优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed training increases the number of batches processed per iteration either by scaling-out (adding more nodes) or scaling-up (increasing the batch-size). However, the largest configuration does not necessarily yield the best performance. Horizontal scaling introduces additional communication overhead, while vertical scaling is constrained by computation cost and device memory limits. Thus, simply increasing the batch-size leads to diminishing returns: training time and cost decrease initially but eventually plateaus, creating a knee-point in the time/cost versus batch-size pareto curve. The optimal batch-size therefore depends on the underlying model, data and available compute resources. Large batches also suffer from worse model quality due to the well-known generalization gap. In this paper, we present Tula, an online service that automatically optimizes time, cost, and convergence quality for large-batch training of convolutional models. It combines parallel-systems modeling with statistical performance prediction to identify the optimal batch-size. Tula predicts training time and cost within 7.5-14% error across multiple models, and achieves up to 20x overall speedup and improves test accuracy by 9% on average over standard large-batch training on various vision tasks, thus successfully mitigating the generalization gap and accelerating training at the same time.

</details>


### [22] [LLM-Augmented Computational Phenotyping of Long Covid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18115)
*Jing Wang,Jie Shen,Amar Sra,Qiaomin Xie,Jeremy C Weiss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种LLM增强的计算表型框架"Grace Cycle"，用于从纵向患者数据中发现Long COVID的临床亚型，成功识别出Protected、Responder和Refractory三种表型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long COVID是一种复杂且持久的疾病，其临床亚型目前尚不清楚。表型表征对于理解慢性疾病的异质性以及指导个性化干预具有重要意义，但现有方法难以从复杂的纵向数据中发现具有临床意义的亚组。

Method: 提出Grace Cycle框架，迭代整合假设生成、证据提取和特征 refinement三个环节，利用大型语言模型增强表型发现的效率和准确性。

Result: 基于13,511名Long COVID参与者识别出三种临床表型：Protected（保护型）、Responder（应答型）和Refractory（难治型）。这些表型在峰值症状严重程度、基线疾病负担和纵向剂量-反应模式方面表现出显著差异，并获得多维度统计支持。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型可有效整合到基于原则的、统计驱动的管道中，用于从复杂纵向数据进行表型筛选。该框架具有疾病无关性，为发现临床可解释的亚表型提供了通用方法。

Abstract: Phenotypic characterization is essential for understanding heterogeneity in chronic diseases and for guiding personalized interventions. Long COVID, a complex and persistent condition, yet its clinical subphenotypes remain poorly understood. In this work, we propose an LLM-augmented computational phenotyping framework ``Grace Cycle'' that iteratively integrates hypothesis generation, evidence extraction, and feature refinement to discover clinically meaningful subgroups from longitudinal patient data. The framework identifies three distinct clinical phenotypes, Protected, Responder, and Refractory, based on 13,511 Long Covid participants. These phenotypes exhibit pronounced separation in peak symptom severity, baseline disease burden, and longitudinal dose-response patterns, with strong statistical support across multiple independent dimensions.
  This study illustrates how large language models can be integrated into a principled, statistically grounded pipeline for phenotypic screening from complex longitudinal data. Note that the proposed framework is disease-agnostic and offers a general approach for discovering clinically interpretable subphenotypes.

</details>


### [23] [R2-Dreamer: Redundancy-Reduced World Models without Decoders or Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18202)
*Naoki Morihira,Amal Nahar,Kartik Bharadwaj,Yasuhiro Kato,Akinobu Hayashi,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: R2-Dreamer提出了一种无解码器的基于模型的强化学习(MBRL)框架,利用类似Barlow Twins的冗余减少目标作为内部正则化器,避免表示崩溃,无需依赖数据增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图像的MBRL面临的核心挑战是学习能够提取关键信息而忽略无关视觉细节的表示。现有的重建方法会浪费容量在大型任务无关区域,而无解码器方法虽能学习鲁棒表示,但依赖数据增强(DA)作为外部正则化器,限制了通用性。

Method: 提出R2-Dreamer,一种无解码器的MBRL框架,核心是引入Barlow Twins启发的冗余减少目标作为内部正则化器,防止表示崩溃,无需借助数据增强。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite和Meta-World上,R2-Dreamer与DreamerV3和TD-MPC2等强基线方法相当,且训练速度比DreamerV3快1.59倍;在DMC-Subtle(任务相关物体很小的场景)中取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 有效的内部正则化器可以实现通用、高性能的无解码器MBRL,无需依赖外部正则化器(如数据增强)。

Abstract: A central challenge in image-based Model-Based Reinforcement Learning (MBRL) is to learn representations that distill essential information from irrelevant visual details. While promising, reconstruction-based methods often waste capacity on large task-irrelevant regions. Decoder-free methods instead learn robust representations by leveraging Data Augmentation (DA), but reliance on such external regularizers limits versatility. We propose R2-Dreamer, a decoder-free MBRL framework with a self-supervised objective that serves as an internal regularizer, preventing representation collapse without resorting to DA. The core of our method is a redundancy-reduction objective inspired by Barlow Twins, which can be easily integrated into existing frameworks. On DeepMind Control Suite and Meta-World, R2-Dreamer is competitive with strong baselines such as DreamerV3 and TD-MPC2 while training 1.59x faster than DreamerV3, and yields substantial gains on DMC-Subtle with tiny task-relevant objects. These results suggest that an effective internal regularizer can enable versatile, high-performance decoder-free MBRL. Code is available at https://github.com/NM512/r2dreamer.

</details>


### [24] [Gradient-Informed Temporal Sampling Improves Rollout Accuracy in PDE Surrogate Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18237)
*Wenshuo Wang,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对神经模拟器的训练数据采样问题，提出了GITS方法，通过联合优化局部梯度和时间覆盖来选择更有效的训练数据，在多种PDE系统和模型骨架上实现了更低的 rollout 误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据采样方法要么陷入高信息密度区域导致过度局部化，要么虽保持多样性但缺乏模型特异性，性能并未优于均匀采样。如何在有限预算下采样训练数据以最大化神经模拟器的 rollout 精度是一个尚未形式化的基本问题。

Method: 提出 Gradient-Informed Temporal Sampling (GITS) 方法，联合优化 pilot-model 的局部梯度和集合级的时间覆盖，同时兼顾模型特异性和动力学信息。

Result: GITS 选出的数据在多种 PDE 系统、模型骨架和采样比例下均达到更低的 rollout 误差。消融实验验证了两个优化目标的必要性和互补性。

Conclusion: GITS 能够有效平衡模型特异性与动力学信息，为神经模拟器的训练数据采样提供了一种有效方案。同时研究还分析了 GITS 成功采样的模式特征，以及其失效的典型 PDE 系统和模型骨架。

Abstract: Researchers train neural simulators on uniformly sampled numerical simulation data. But under the same budget, does systematically sampled data provide the most effective information? A fundamental yet unformalized problem is how to sample training data for neural simulators so as to maximize rollout accuracy. Existing data sampling methods either tend to collapse into locally high-information-density regions, or preserve diversity but remain insufficiently model-specific, often leading to performance that is no better than uniform sampling. To address this, we propose a data sampling method tailored to neural simulators, Gradient-Informed Temporal Sampling (GITS). GITS jointly optimizes pilot-model local gradients and set-level temporal coverage, thereby effectively balancing model specificity and dynamical information. Compared with multiple sampling baselines, the data selected by GITS achieves lower rollout error across multiple PDE systems, model backbones and sample ratios. Furthermore, ablation studies demonstrate the necessity and complementarity of the two optimization objectives in GITS. In addition, we analyze the successful sampling patterns of GITS as well as the typical PDE systems and model backbones on which GITS fails.

</details>


### [25] [AGRI-Fidelity: Evaluating the Reliability of Listenable Explanations for Poultry Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18247)
*Sindhuja Madabushi,Arda Dogan,Jonathan Liu,Dian Chen,Dong S. Ha,Sook Shin,Sam H. Noh,Jin-Hee Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AGRI-Fidelity是一种针对家禽疾病检测中可解释性音频解释的可靠性评估框架，针对现有XAI指标仅关注单一模型忠实度而忽视模型多样性问题，以及噪声农场环境中基于掩码的指标无法惩罚冗余捷径的问题。该方法结合跨模型共识和循环时间置换构建零分布，计算假发现率(FDR)以抑制 stationary artifacts同时保留时间局部化的生物声学标记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 现有XAI指标仅测量单一模型的忠实度，忽视了模型多样性（near-optimal classifiers依赖不同或伪 acoustic cues）
2. 噪声农场环境中，stationary artifacts（如通风噪声）产生忠实但不可靠的解释
3. 基于掩码的指标无法惩罚冗余捷径，导致在无空间ground truth的情况下无法提供可靠评估

Method: 1. 结合跨模型共识（cross-model consensus）与循环时间置换（cyclic temporal permutation）
2. 构建零分布来计算假发现率（FDR）
3. 目标：抑制stationary artifacts同时保留时间局部化的生物声学标记

Result: 在真实和受控数据集上，AGRI-Fidelity能够为所有数据点提供可靠性感知区分，优于基于掩码的指标

Conclusion: AGRI-Fidelity作为一个可靠性导向的评估框架，在无需空间ground truth的家禽疾病检测可解释性评估中有效解决了stationary artifacts问题

Abstract: Existing XAI metrics measure faithfulness for a single model, ignoring model multiplicity where near-optimal classifiers rely on different or spurious acoustic cues. In noisy farm environments, stationary artifacts such as ventilation noise can produce explanations that are faithful yet unreliable, as masking-based metrics fail to penalize redundant shortcuts. We propose AGRI-Fidelity, a reliability-oriented evaluation framework for listenable explanations in poultry disease detection without spatial ground truth. The method combines cross-model consensus with cyclic temporal permutation to construct null distributions and compute a False Discovery Rate (FDR), suppressing stationary artifacts while preserving time-localized bioacoustic markers. Across real and controlled datasets, AGRI-Fidelity effectively provides reliability-aware discrimination for all data points versus masking-based metrics.

</details>


### [26] [MolRGen: A Training and Evaluation Setting for De Novo Molecular Generation with Reasonning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18256)
*Philippe Formont,Maxime Darrin,Ismail Ben Ayed,Pablo Piantanida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MolRGen是一个用于训练和评估基于推理的大型语言模型进行从头分子生成的大规模基准数据集和 benchmark。针对现有方法依赖监督标签无法适用于de novo分子生成的问题，该研究提出了评估训练设置、多样性感知top-k评分指标，并用强化学习训练了24B参数的LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于推理的大型语言模型在复杂问题解决任务中展现出显著进步，多个工作探索了其在药物发现和分子设计中的应用。然而现有方法要么聚焦于评估，要么依赖于需要 ground-truth 标签的训练设置（如已知属性修饰的分子对）。这种监督在de novo分子生成中不可用，其目标是在没有高评分候选分子先验知识的情况下生成分子来优化期望分数。

Method: 1) 提出评估和训练de novo分子生成与属性预测的设置；2) 引入多样性感知top-k评分（diversity-aware top-k score），同时捕捉生成分子的质量和多样性；3) 使用强化学习训练24B参数的LLM模型。

Result: 展示了该设置可用于训练LLM进行分子生成，并提供了24B LLM的性能和局限性详细分析。

Conclusion: 成功构建了MolRGen基准，为基于推理的LLM在de novo分子生成任务上的训练和评估提供了标准化的环境和评价指标，推动了LLM在药物发现领域的应用。

Abstract: Recent advances in reasoning-based large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated substantial improvements in complex problem-solving tasks. Motivated by these advances, several works have explored the application of reasoning LLMs to drug discovery and molecular design. However, most existing approaches either focus on evaluation or rely on training setups that require ground-truth labels, such as molecule pairs with known property modifications. Such supervision is unavailable in \textit{de novo} molecular generation, where the objective is to generate novel molecules that optimize a desirability score without prior knowledge of high-scoring candidates. To bridge this gap, we introduce MolRGen, a large-scale benchmark and dataset for training and evaluating reasoning-based LLMs on \textit{de novo} molecular generation. Our contributions are threefold. First, we propose a setting to evaluate and train models for \textit{de novo} molecular generation and property prediction. Second, we introduce a novel diversity-aware top-$k$ score that captures both the quality and diversity of generated molecules. Third, we show our setting can be used to train LLMs for molecular generation, training a 24B LLM with reinforcement learning, and we provide a detailed analysis of its performance and limitations.

</details>


### [27] [Sharpness-Aware Minimization in Logit Space Efficiently Enhances Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18258)
*Haocheng Luo,Zehang Deng,Thanh-Toan Do,Mehrtash Harandi,Dinh Phung,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了直接偏好优化(DPO)算法中的"挤压效应"(squeezing effect)问题,并提出使用logits-SAM方法来缓解这一问题。理论分析表明,负梯度更新导致残差沿高曲率方向快速扩展,从而引发挤压效应,而SAM可以通过其曲率正则化效应抑制该行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO算法虽因其简单性和训练稳定性而广泛用于大语言模型与人类偏好的对齐,但存在挤压效应问题——训练过程中首选响应的概率会非预期地下降,这严重影响了模型的对齐效果。现有方法缺乏对这一现象的理论解释和有效解决方案。

Method: 1. 建立理论框架分析logit空间的坐标级动态行为;2. 识别负梯度更新导致高曲率方向残差快速扩展是挤压效应的根本原因;3. 引入Sharpness-Aware Minimization(SAM)通过曲率正则化抑制该行为;4. 提出计算高效的logits-SAM变体,仅对输出层进行扰动,开销极低。

Result: 在Pythia-2.8B、Mistral-7B和Gemma-2B-IT模型上,跨多个数据集和基准的广泛实验表明,logits-SAM能持续提升DPO的有效性,并与其他DPO变体无缝集成。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析和实验验证,logits-SAM成功解决了DPO的挤压效应问题,为语言模型对齐提供了一种简单有效的训练策略。该方法计算开销小、易于集成,具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has emerged as a popular algorithm for aligning pretrained large language models with human preferences, owing to its simplicity and training stability. However, DPO suffers from the recently identified squeezing effect (also known as likelihood displacement), where the probability of preferred responses decreases unintentionally during training. To understand and mitigate this phenomenon, we develop a theoretical framework that models the coordinate-wise dynamics in logit space. Our analysis reveals that negative-gradient updates cause residuals to expand rapidly along high-curvature directions, which underlies the squeezing effect, whereas Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) can suppress this behavior through its curvature-regularization effect. Building on this insight, we investigate logits-SAM, a computationally efficient variant that perturbs only the output layer with negligible overhead. Extensive experiments on Pythia-2.8B, Mistral-7B, and Gemma-2B-IT across multiple datasets and benchmarks demonstrate that logits-SAM consistently improves the effectiveness of DPO and integrates seamlessly with other DPO variants. Code is available at https://github.com/RitianLuo/logits-sam-dpo.

</details>


### [28] [Detection Is Cheap, Routing Is Learned: Why Refusal-Based Alignment Evaluation Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18280)
*Gregory N. Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究中国来源语言模型中的政治审查机制，提出当前对齐评估仅关注概念检测和拒绝行为，忽略了"路由"这一关键中间层。作者通过探针、手术消融和行为测试对来自5个实验室的9个开源模型进行研究，发现：1) 探针准确率本身无法诊断问题，需要进行类别泛化测试；2) 手术消融揭示了实验室特定的路由机制；3) 拒绝不再是主要的审查方式，叙事引导取而代之。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对齐评估主要衡量模型是否编码危险概念以及是否拒绝有害请求，但这两种方法都忽略了对齐操作的中间层——从概念检测到行为策略的路由过程。作者以中国语言模型的政治审查为自然实验，试图揭示这一被忽视的机制。

Method: 研究采用三种方法：1) 探针(Probes)用于检测政治敏感概念；2) 手术消融(Surgical ablation)移除政治敏感方向；3) 跨9个来自5个实验室的开源模型进行行为测试。

Result: 三个主要发现：1) 探针准确率本身无法诊断问题——政治探针、空对照和排列基线均可达到100%，因此留出类别泛化是有效测试；2) 手术消融揭示了实验室特定的路由机制——移除政治敏感方向可消除审查并恢复准确的事实输出，但一个模型因架构纠缠了事实知识与审查机制而产生虚构；3) 拒绝不再是主要的审查机制——在一个模型家族中，硬拒绝降至零而叙事引导升至最高，使审查对仅检测拒绝的基准不可见。

Conclusion: 本文提出三阶段描述框架：检测→路由→生成。模型通常保留相关知识，对齐改变的是知识的表达方式。仅审计检测或拒绝的评估会错过最直接决定行为的路由机制。未来的对齐评估应关注路由层面的几何结构。

Abstract: Current alignment evaluation mostly measures whether models encode dangerous concepts and whether they refuse harmful requests. Both miss the layer where alignment often operates: routing from concept detection to behavioral policy. We study political censorship in Chinese-origin language models as a natural experiment, using probes, surgical ablations, and behavioral tests across nine open-weight models from five labs. Three findings follow. First, probe accuracy alone is non-diagnostic: political probes, null controls, and permutation baselines can all reach 100%, so held-out category generalization is the informative test. Second, surgical ablation reveals lab-specific routing. Removing the political-sensitivity direction eliminates censorship and restores accurate factual output in most models tested, while one model confabulates because its architecture entangles factual knowledge with the censorship mechanism. Cross-model transfer fails, indicating that routing geometry is model- and lab-specific. Third, refusal is no longer the dominant censorship mechanism. Within one model family, hard refusal falls to zero while narrative steering rises to the maximum, making censorship invisible to refusal-only benchmarks. These results support a three-stage descriptive framework: detect, route, generate. Models often retain the relevant knowledge; alignment changes how that knowledge is expressed. Evaluations that audit only detection or refusal therefore miss the routing mechanism that most directly determines behavior.

</details>

*Simon M. Brealy,Lawrence A. Bull,Daniel S. Brennan,Pauline Beltrando,Anders Sommer,Nikolaos Dervilis,Keith Worden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从群体层面视角出发，研究利用加性高斯过程揭示风力发电机组和风电场级别功率模型的变异规律，通过收集的风电场数据集进行预测验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统结构健康监测主要关注单一机器，而PBSHM旨在共享相似机器或结构之间的信息，以实现更优的监控和决策。

Method: 采用加性高斯过程方法建立群体层面的功率模型，能够捕捉风电机组特异性和场级特征的变异。

Result: 预测结果展示了风电场功率生成的模式，这些模式符合直觉，可为控制和决策提供更充分的信息支持。

Conclusion: 加性高斯过程方法能够有效揭示风电机组和风电场级别的功率变异规律，具有应用于PBSHM的潜力。

Abstract: Population-based Structural Health Monitoring (PBSHM) aims to share information between similar machines or structures. This paper takes a population-level perspective, exploring the use of additive Gaussian processes to reveal variations in turbine-specific and farm-level power models over a collected wind farm dataset. The predictions illustrate patterns in wind farm power generation, which follow intuition and should enable more informed control and decision-making.

</details>


### [30] [Path-Constrained Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18297)
*Zijin Gu,Tatiana Likhomanenko,Vimal Thilak,Jason Ramapuram,Navdeep Jaitly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PathMoE通过跨层共享路由参数来解决稀疏MoE架构中路径空间过大(N^L)导致的统计效率问题，在0.9B和16B模型上验证了其在困惑度和下游任务上的改进，并揭示了专家路径与语言功能之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE路由在每层独立选择专家，导致可能的专家路径数量达到N^L（远超训练集规模），模型难以学习有意义的路径结构，造成统计效率低下。

Method: 提出PathMoE，跨连续层共享路由参数，约束路径空间，使每层使用相同的路由决策逻辑。

Result: 在0.9B和16B参数模型上，PathMoE相比独立路由在困惑度和下游任务上均取得一致提升，无需辅助负载均衡损失。分析表明：遵循相同路径的token按语言功能自然聚类，PathMoE产生更集中的分组、更强的跨层一致性，对路由扰动更鲁棒。

Conclusion: PathMoE为理解MoE架构提供了"专家路径"的新视角，跨层参数共享是简单而有效的约束路径空间的方法。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures enable efficient scaling by activating only a subset of parameters for each input. However, conventional MoE routing selects each layer's experts independently, creating N^L possible expert paths -- for N experts across L layers. This far exceeds typical training set sizes, leading to statistical inefficiency as the model may not learn meaningful structure over such a vast path space. To constrain it, we propose \pathmoe, which shares router parameters across consecutive layers. Experiments on 0.9B and 16B parameter models demonstrate consistent improvements on perplexity and downstream tasks over independent routing, while eliminating the need for auxiliary load balancing losses. Analysis reveals that tokens following the same path naturally cluster by linguistic function, with \pathmoe{} producing more concentrated groups, better cross-layer consistency, and greater robustness to routing perturbations. These results offer a new perspective for understanding MoE architectures through the lens of expert paths.

</details>


### [31] [Learning to Reason with Curriculum I: Provable Benefits of Autocurriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18325)
*Nived Rajaraman,Audrey Huang,Miro Dudik,Robert Schapire,Dylan J. Foster,Akshay Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出autocurriculum方法，通过让模型自适应选择训练数据来降低链式思考推理模型的训练成本。理论证明该方法在SFT中需要指数级更少样本，在RL中可将参考模型质量要求降低为独立的burn-in成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练链式思考推理模型成本极高，需要收集大量人工或合成的推理轨迹，并通过强化学习进行后训练。文章旨在探讨这些成本是否为不可避免的fundamental问题，或者可以通过更好的算法设计来降低。

Method: 提出autocurriculum方法，即模型利用自身表现来决定训练时应该关注哪些问题。该方法结合了boosting和从反例中学习的经典技术，通过自适应数据选择来提高训练效率。

Result: 对于监督微调(SFT)，autocurriculum相比非自适应微调需要指数级更少的推理演示样本；对于强化学习微调(RL)，autocurriculum将计算成本与参考模型质量解耦，使后者降低为几乎与目标精度无关的burn-in成本。

Conclusion: 这些改进纯粹来自于自适应数据选择，不需要对prompt的分布或难度做任何假设，为降低推理模型训练成本提供了有效的算法解决方案。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning, where language models expend additional computation by producing thinking tokens prior to final responses, has driven significant advances in model capabilities. However, training these reasoning models is extremely costly in terms of both data and compute, as it involves collecting long traces of reasoning behavior from humans or synthetic generators and further post-training the model via reinforcement learning. Are these costs fundamental, or can they be reduced through better algorithmic design? We show that autocurriculum, where the model uses its own performance to decide which problems to focus training on, provably improves upon standard training recipes for both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL). For SFT, we show that autocurriculum requires exponentially fewer reasoning demonstrations than non-adaptive fine-tuning, by focusing teacher supervision on prompts where the current model struggles. For RL fine-tuning, autocurriculum decouples the computational cost from the quality of the reference model, reducing the latter to a burn-in cost that is nearly independent of the target accuracy. These improvements arise purely from adaptive data selection, drawing on classical techniques from boosting and learning from counterexamples, and requiring no assumption on the distribution or difficulty of prompts.

</details>


### [32] [Escaping Offline Pessimism: Vector-Field Reward Shaping for Safe Frontier Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18326)
*Amirhossein Roknilamouki,Arnob Ghosh,Eylem Ekici,Ness B. Shroff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对离线强化学习的边界探索方法，通过向量场奖励塑形来解决离线策略部署后的探索受限问题。该方法结合梯度对齐项和旋转流项，引导智能体在不确定性边界处进行持续、安全的探索，避免退化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习虽然能提供可靠的部署策略，但其固有的悲观主义严重限制了智能体在线探索和收集新数据的能力。借鉴安全强化学习思想，在离线数据集覆盖良好且模拟器可信赖的区域边界附近进行探索，可以让智能体在可管理风险下获取信息，同时保持安全恢复能力。

Method: 提出一种向量场奖励塑形范式，包含两个互补组件：1) 梯度对齐项，将智能体吸引到目标不确定性水平；2) 旋转流项，促进智能体沿不确定性流形的局部切平面运动。该方法基于离线数据训练的不确定性预言机运作。

Result: 在2D连续导航任务中与Soft Actor-Critic集成验证表明，智能体成功穿越不确定性边界，同时平衡了安全信息收集与主要任务完成。

Conclusion: 该奖励结构能从理论上自然诱导边界处的持续探索行为并防止退化解。实验验证了该方法在离线策略部署后进行安全、信息化数据收集的可行性。

Abstract: While offline reinforcement learning provides reliable policies for real-world deployment, its inherent pessimism severely restricts an agent's ability to explore and collect novel data online. Drawing inspiration from safe reinforcement learning, exploring near the boundary of regions well covered by the offline dataset and reliably modeled by the simulator allows an agent to take manageable risks--venturing into informative but moderate-uncertainty states while remaining close enough to familiar regions for safe recovery. However, naively rewarding this boundary-seeking behavior can lead to a degenerate parking behavior, where the agent simply stops once it reaches the frontier. To solve this, we propose a novel vector-field reward shaping paradigm designed to induce continuous, safe boundary exploration for non-adaptive deployed policies. Operating on an uncertainty oracle trained from offline data, our reward combines two complementary components: a gradient-alignment term that attracts the agent toward a target uncertainty level, and a rotational-flow term that promotes motion along the local tangent plane of the uncertainty manifold. Through theoretical analysis, we show that this reward structure naturally induces sustained exploratory behavior along the boundary while preventing degenerate solutions. Empirically, by integrating our proposed reward shaping with Soft Actor-Critic on a 2D continuous navigation task, we validate that agents successfully traverse uncertainty boundaries while balancing safe, informative data collection with primary task completion.

</details>


### [33] [FlowMS: Flow Matching for De Novo Structure Elucidation from Mass Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18397)
*Jianan Nie,Peng Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowMS是首个将离散流匹配应用于质谱条件下的分子生成任务的方法，通过迭代细化概率空间生成分子图，并强制执行化学式约束，在NPLIB1基准上实现了5/6指标的先进水平，top-1准确率达9.15%，top-10 MCES为7.96。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质谱(MS)是分子鉴定的核心分析技术，但从光谱进行de novo结构解析仍具挑战性，原因在于化学空间的组合复杂性和光谱碎裂模式的固有模糊性。现有的深度学习方法包括自回归序列模型、基于支架的方法和图扩散模型，但扩散模型计算量大，且离散流匹配在图生成中表现出色但尚未应用于光谱条件下的结构解析。

Method: FlowMS是首个离散流匹配框架，用于光谱条件下的de novo分子生成。通过概率空间的迭代细化生成分子图，同时强制执行化学式约束，并利用预训练的formula transformer编码器对光谱嵌入进行条件化处理。

Result: 在NPLIB1基准上实现了5/6指标的先进水平：top-1准确率达9.15%（比DiffMS相对提升9.7%），top-10 MCES为7.96（比MS-BART提升4.2%）。生成的可视化分子表明FlowMS能产生与真实结构高度相似的结构合理的候选分子。

Conclusion: 离散流匹配作为质谱结构解析的新范式，在代谢组学和天然产物发现领域具有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: Mass spectrometry (MS) stands as a cornerstone analytical technique for molecular identification, yet de novo structure elucidation from spectra remains challenging due to the combinatorial complexity of chemical space and the inherent ambiguity of spectral fragmentation patterns. Recent deep learning approaches, including autoregressive sequence models, scaffold-based methods, and graph diffusion models, have made progress. However, diffusion-based generation for this task remains computationally demanding. Meanwhile, discrete flow matching, which has shown strong performance for graph generation, has not yet been explored for spectrum-conditioned structure elucidation. In this work, we introduce FlowMS, the first discrete flow matching framework for spectrum-conditioned de novo molecular generation. FlowMS generates molecular graphs through iterative refinement in probability space, enforcing chemical formula constraints while conditioning on spectral embeddings from a pretrained formula transformer encoder. Notably, it achieves state-of-the-art performance on 5 out of 6 metrics on the NPLIB1 benchmark: 9.15% top-1 accuracy (9.7% relative improvement over DiffMS) and 7.96 top-10 MCES (4.2% improvement over MS-BART). We also visualize the generated molecules, which further demonstrate that FlowMS produces structurally plausible candidates closely resembling ground truth structures. These results establish discrete flow matching as a promising paradigm for mass spectrometry-based structure elucidation in metabolomics and natural product discovery.

</details>


### [34] [Self-Tuning Sparse Attention: Multi-Fidelity Hyperparameter Optimization for Transformer Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18417)
*Arundhathi Dev,Justin Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AFBS-BO是一种自适应 fidelity 的贝叶斯优化框架，用于自动发现稀疏注意力机制的最优层和头特定超参数，无需人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏注意力机制有望打破长上下文transformers的二次瓶颈，但生产采用受到关键可用性问题的限制：最优超参数在不同层和模型之间差异很大，现有方法依赖人工网格搜索来识别。

Method: 提出AFBS-BO（自适应 fidelity 二分搜索与贝叶斯优化），结合贝叶斯优化进行全局探索和二分搜索进行局部细化，跨序列长度利用多 fidelity 评估来降低调优成本。

Result: 在Llama-2-7B上，AFBS-BO加速超参数发现3.4倍，评估次数比网格搜索少8.8倍，识别出优于现有稀疏注意力基线的高稀疏度配置，同时紧密匹配密集注意力质量。

Conclusion: AFBS-BO将稀疏注意力从手动调优的启发式方法转变为自优化原语，能够在不同的transformer架构和领域实现即插即用的加速。

Abstract: Sparse attention mechanisms promise to break the quadratic bottleneck of long-context transformers, yet production adoption remains limited by a critical usability gap: optimal hyperparameters vary substantially across layers and models, and current methods (e.g., SpargeAttn) rely on manual grid search to identify them. We propose AFBS-BO (Adaptive Fidelity Binary Search with Bayesian Optimization), a fully automated framework that discovers optimal layer- and head-specific hyperparameters without human intervention. Our hybrid algorithm combines Bayesian Optimization for global exploration with binary search for local refinement, leveraging multi-fidelity evaluation across sequence lengths to reduce tuning cost. On Llama-2-7B, AFBS-BO accelerates hyperparameter discovery by 3.4x with 8.8x fewer evaluations than grid search, and identifies high-sparsity configurations that outperform existing sparse attention baselines while closely matching dense attention quality. By transforming sparse attention from a manually tuned heuristic into a self-optimizing primitive, AFBS-BO enables plug-and-play acceleration across diverse transformer architectures and domains.

</details>


### [35] [Towards Noise-Resilient Quantum Multi-Armed and Stochastic Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18431)
*Zhuoyue Chen,Kechao Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究噪声中间规模量子(NISQ)设备下的量子多臂老虎机(QMAB)和量子随机线性老虎机(QSLB)算法，提出噪声鲁棒的量子蒙特卡洛估计器，在保持量子优势的同时提升噪声环境下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子MAB算法假设在无噪声电路上使用理想量子蒙特卡洛(QMC)程序，忽略了当前NISQ设备上噪声的影响，需要研究噪声鲁棒的算法。

Method: 1. 开发噪声鲁棒的QMC算法以提升量子奖励oracle的估计精度；2. 基于该估计器构建噪声鲁棒的QMAB和QSLB算法。

Result: 实验表明，噪声鲁棒方法在多种量子噪声模型下提升了QMAB估计精度并降低了后悔值。

Conclusion: 所提噪声鲁棒算法在 NISQ 噪声环境下能保持对经典方法的量子优势，为量子强化学习在真实硬件上的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Quantum multi-armed bandits (MAB) and stochastic linear bandits (SLB) have recently attracted significant attention, as their quantum counterparts can achieve quadratic speedups over classical MAB and SLB. However, most existing quantum MAB algorithms assume ideal quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) procedures on noise-free circuits, overlooking the impact of noise in current noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices. In this paper, we study a noise-robust QMC algorithm that improves estimation accuracy when querying quantum reward oracles. Building on this estimator, we propose noise-robust QMAB and QSLB algorithms that enhance performance in noisy environments while preserving the advantage over classical methods. Experiments show that our noise-robust approach improves QMAB estimation accuracy and reduces regret under several quantum noise models.

</details>

*Runze Yang,Longbing Cao,Xiaoming Wu,Xin You,Kun Fang,Jianxun Li,Jie Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MLOW是一种新的基于频率的可解释时间序列分解管道，通过将时间序列表示为幅度谱与相位感知基函数的乘积，实现多效应分解。该方法学习幅度谱的低秩表示来捕捉主导的趋势和季节效应，并提出超平面非负矩阵分解(Hyperplane-NMF)来解决现有低秩方法无法同时实现可解释、高效和可泛化分解的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测模型无法通过其基于平滑的时间技术有效学习可解释的多效应分解。时间序列的幅度谱分布对不同效应表现出可观察的模式，这为分解提供了新的思路。

Method: 1)将时间序列表示为幅度谱乘以相位感知基函数；2)学习幅度谱的低秩表示以捕捉趋势和季节效应；3)探索PCA、NMF、Semi-NMF等低秩方法；4)提出Hyperplane-NMF解决现有方法的局限性；5)通过数学机制实现输入视野和频率水平的灵活选择，解决频谱泄漏问题。

Result: MLOW实现了可解释的层次化多效应分解，对噪声具有鲁棒性；可作为即插即用模块集成到现有预测框架中，以最小的架构修改获得显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 基于频率的分解方法为时间序列预测中的多效应分离提供了一种有效且可解释的新范式，Hyperplane-NMF弥补了现有低秩分解方法的不足。

Abstract: Separating multiple effects in time series is fundamental yet challenging for time-series forecasting (TSF). However, existing TSF models cannot effectively learn interpretable multi-effect decomposition by their smoothing-based temporal techniques. Here, a new interpretable frequency-based decomposition pipeline MLOW captures the insight: a time series can be represented as a magnitude spectrum multiplied by the corresponding phase-aware basis functions, and the magnitude spectrum distribution of a time series always exhibits observable patterns for different effects. MLOW learns a low-rank representation of the magnitude spectrum to capture dominant trending and seasonal effects. We explore low-rank methods, including PCA, NMF, and Semi-NMF, and find that none can simultaneously achieve interpretable, efficient and generalizable decomposition. Thus, we propose hyperplane-nonnegative matrix factorization (Hyperplane-NMF). Further, to address the frequency (spectral) leakage restricting high-quality low-rank decomposition, MLOW enables a flexible selection of input horizons and frequency levels via a mathematical mechanism. Visual analysis demonstrates that MLOW enables interpretable and hierarchical multiple-effect decomposition, robust to noises. It can also enable plug-and-play in existing TSF backbones with remarkable performance improvement but minimal architectural modifications.

</details>


### [37] [Discounted Beta--Bernoulli Reward Estimation for Sample-Efficient Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18444)
*Haechan Kim,Soohyun Ryu,Gyouk Chu,Doohyuk Jang,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的强化学习奖励估计方法DBB（Discounted Beta-Bernoulli），通过将RLVR从点估计问题重新表述为分布估计问题，显著提升了group-based RLVR的样本效率，在1.7B和8B模型上分别实现了ID 3.22/2.42和OOD 12.49/6.92的Acc@8提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有group-based RLVR方法存在严重的样本效率问题，根源在于：1）依赖少量rollout的点估计；2）高估计方差；3）方差崩溃；4）生成响应利用不充分。这些问题导致训练不稳定且样本浪费严重。

Method: 1）将奖励建模为策略诱导分布的样本，将优势估计问题转化为有限数据下的奖励分布估计问题；2）提出Discounted Beta-Bernoulli（DBB）奖励估计器，利用历史奖励统计数据估计非平稳分布；3）该估计器虽 有偏但方差更小且稳定，理论上避免方差崩溃， MSE低于标准点估计。

Result: 在6个ID和3个OOD推理基准上，DBB-GRPO相比naive GRPO：1.7B模型ID提升3.22分，OOD提升12.49分；8B模型ID提升2.42分，OOD提升6.92分；无额外计算或内存开销。

Conclusion: DBB通过统计估计视角重新建模RLVR，有效解决了group-based方法的样本效率问题，在保持零额外开销的前提下显著提升了分布内外的推理性能，为LLM后训练提供了一种高效且可扩展的奖励估计方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as an effective post-training paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models. However, existing group-based RLVR methods often suffer from severe sample inefficiency. This inefficiency stems from reliance on point estimation of rewards from a small number of rollouts, leading to high estimation variance, variance collapse, and ineffective utilization of generated responses. In this work, we reformulate RLVR from a statistical estimation perspective by modeling rewards as samples drawn from a policy-induced distribution and casting advantage computation as the problem of estimating the reward distribution from finite data. Building on this view, we propose Discounted Beta--Bernoulli (DBB) reward estimation, which leverages historical reward statistics for the non-stationary distribution. Although biased, the resulting estimator exhibits reduced and stable variance, theoretically avoids estimated variance collapse, and achieves lower mean squared error than standard point estimation. Extensive experiments across six in-distribution and three out-of-distribution reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that GRPO with DBB consistently outperforms naive GRPO, achieving average Acc@8 improvements of 3.22/2.42 points in-distribution and 12.49/6.92 points out-of-distribution on the 1.7B and 8B models, respectively, without additional computational cost or memory usage.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了SLU-SUITE，一个包含49万条人工标注问答对的电影镜头语言理解训练和评估套件，涵盖33个任务和6个电影维度。基于该套件发现VLM与电影专家判断存在差异，进而提出两种解决方案：UniShot（通用模型）和AgentShots（专家集群），在域内和域外任务上均取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电影分析中的镜头语言理解（SLU）因多样化的电影制作维度和主观专家判断而具有挑战性。尽管视觉语言模型（VLM）在通用视觉理解方面表现强劲，但研究表明VLM与电影专家在SLU任务上存在判断差异，需要解决这一gap。

Method: 1）构建SLU-SUITE：49万QA对，33任务，6电影维度；2）诊断分析：从模型侧定位模块瓶颈，从数据侧量化跨维度影响；3）UniShot：动态平衡数据混合训练的统一模型；4）AgentShots：提示路由的专家集群

Result: 域内任务上优于任务特定集成方法；域外任务上超越领先商业VLM达22%。

Conclusion: 通过大规模电影领域专用数据集和dual-paradigm方法（通用模型+专家集群），有效解决了VLM与电影专家在镜头语言理解上的判断差异问题，为电影AI分析提供了新范式。

Abstract: Shot language understanding (SLU) is crucial for cinematic analysis but remains challenging due to its diverse cinematographic dimensions and subjective expert judgment. While vision-language models (VLMs) have shown strong ability in general visual understanding, recent studies reveal judgment discrepancies between VLMs and film experts on SLU tasks.
